Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the fermi-dirac object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 19. The moduli space has dimension at most 541 with 2 connected components.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by fermi-dirac generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 19, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 541. QED.

Theorem 212.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For any fermi-dirac module M satisfying the structural decomposition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 573. The projective resolution has length at most 591 with Betti numbers bounded by 2.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the structural decomposition condition on fermi-dirac structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 573. The convergence follows from the structural decomposition estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 591. QED.
